For provisions related to setbacks for projected portions at roof level, open spaces and
courtyards, projections and balconies, parking requirements, exemptions, general notes, overall
building requirements, occupant load, clearance zones, part building requirements such as; plinth
levels, spatial requirements for various building components, lighting and ventilation of rooms,
ledges or tand, mezzanine floors, basements, chimneys, parapets, cabins, boundary walls, septic
tanks, office-cum-letter box rooms, meter rooms, staircases and exit routes, corridor dimensions,
ramps, lift and escalator, roof structures, sunken courtyards, check posts or guard rooms or
watch-and-ward cabins, and drainage and sanitation fitments, refer to rules 39 to 43 of these
building rules.
Further, for the provisions of Fire protection, Structural design, Lighting and ventilation,
Electrical and allied installations and Building Materials refer to part VI of these building rules.
Furthermore, for the provisions of water conservation and management, energy
efficiency, waste management and energy utilization, Energy efficiency, Solar photovoltaic,
Rainwater harvesting and Sustainable waste management refer to part VIII of these building
rules.
Note:-
(a) In case of projects, colonies, or urban estates that have already been approved,
additional Ground Coverage and FAR (Floor Area Ratio) may be permitted within the
rear and side setbacks, but not within the front setback, in order to maintain the existing
building line. For this purpose, there shall be no requirement to revise the existing zoning
plan of the approved project, colony, or urban estate.
However, if the additional Ground Coverage and FAR are proposed to be utilized
within the front setback, the zoning plan of the project, colony, or urban estate shall be
got amended by the promoter, RWA (Resident Welfare Association), or development
authority, as applicable.
Further, it is clarified that if a building has already been constructed and is not in
conformity with the approved plans, although it conforms to these building rules, there
shall be no requirement to compound it separately. Such buildings may be validated
under these rules by charging a fee for the additional Ground Coverage and FAR as
specified in Annexure 22 along with other charges including scrutiny fee, labour cess etc
as applicable.
(b) Each floor of a residential plot may be considered a separate independent floor.
(c) The stilt area shall be used exclusively for car or scooter parking, except for staircases
and lifts.
(d) Basement, permitted only as single level, if constructed, may be used for parking,
household storage, habitable except sleeping accommodation or residential purpose and
consultancy service purposes. Basement shall be counted towards Floor Area Ratio, if
used for habitable purposes.
(e) Front boundary wall shall be optional for providing easy access for parking in front
setback within plot area.
(f) No opening is allowed on party wall of properties.
(g) The maximum permissible ground coverage and Floor Area Ratio is subject to the
fulfilment of the setback, height and parking norms.
(h) A toilet, not exceeding 6 sq.m in area and 2.75 m in height, may be permitted on the
terrace and shall be excluded from the FAR calculation.

(a) The stilt area shall be used exclusively for car or scooter parking, except for staircases
and lifts.
(b) No Basement shall be permitted.
(c) Front boundary wall shall be optional for providing easy access for parking in front
setback within plot area.
(d) No opening is allowed on party wall of properties.
(e) A toilet, not exceeding 6sq.m in area and 2.75 m in height, may be permitted on the
terrace and shall be excluded from the FAR calculation.
(f) Common staircase is permissible maximum for two plots subject to the condition that the
minimum clear width of staircases shall be 900 mm.

sizes, kitchen, toilet, and other spaces, shall be followed as per Sr. No. XIII of Table 6

cum-letter box rooms, meter rooms, staircases and exit routes, corridor dimensions, ramps, lift and

Note:-
(a) The ground coverage and Floor Area Ratio is inclusive of ancillary buildings
such as labour or servant quarters which shall not be more than 25 percent of the
Floor Area Ratio area of the site.
(b) The temporary structure or building like Cattle Shed, Motor Room, covered
parking etc. shall be exempted from ground coverage and floor area ratio.
(c) No sub-division of plot shall be allowed.
(d) Basement upto building line, limited to single level only, if constructed, may be
used for parking, household storage and habitable except sleeping
accommodation purposes. Basement shall be counted towards Floor Area Ratio,
if used for habitable purposes.
(e) Check Post or guard room or watch and ward cabins, whatever the term used,
upto 150 square feet may be allowed along the entry gate and exempted from
Ground Coverage and Floor Area Ratio (FAR). The total height of the guard
room from plinth shall not be more than 2.75 meters.
(2) Other general control for residential building
(i) For the purpose of calculating the infrastructure requirements of the project, the
population shall be estimated based on the type of residential unit. The population
for villas shall be counted at 4.5 persons per villa. For residential plots without
stilt (G+2) or with stilt +3 floors, the population shall be counted at 13.5 persons
per plot, while for residential plots with stilt +4 floors, it shall be 18 persons per
plot. In the case of group housing, the population shall be counted at 4.5 persons
per dwelling unit and in case of Studio Apartments, it shall be counted as two
persons per unit.
(ii) Floating population shall be taken as minimum one hundred person per acre for
calculation of infrastructural or services requirements only and shall not be
counted towards the regular density of the project or sector.
(iii) The density for each category of flats in case of Group Housing or Apartments or
Studio Apartments shall be calculated separately. The promoter has to clearly
indicate the area under each category of flats.
(iv) The clubbing or amalgamation of two or more adjoining residential plots sites
may be permitted, subject to the approval of a revised zoning plan by the
Competent Authority. Whereas, bifurcation or fragmentation of a plot shall not be
allowed. However, in case of approved standalone site or project, the transfer of
Change of Land Use (CLU) or approval of the entire site or part thereof (subject
to the fulfilment of minimum area and other siting guidelines) may be allowed
and shall be governed by the relevant policies of the department.
(v) The clubbing or amalgamation of two or more adjoining Group Housing sites of
same promoter or developer may be permitted, subject to the approval of a revised
zoning plan by the Competent Authority. However, bifurcation or fragmentation
of a site shall not be allowed.
(vi) For Low Density Country Homes Residential Development Projects, Economical
Weaker Section housing, Affordable housing, housing for all, Pardhan Mantri
Awas Yojna (PMAY), Punjab Shehri Awas Yojana (PSAY) and any other policy
relating to housing issued by State or Central Government, the physical and

1706 PUNJAB GOVT. GAZ. (EXTRA), DECEMBER 15, 2025 (AGHN 24, 1947 SAKA)
development norms as prescribed in the respective policies as amended from time
to time, shall be applicable.
(vii) Professional activity shall be allowed in residential plots and flats on any floor,
subject to the following condition: -
Part of the premises shall be permitted to be used upto a maximum of 50
percent of Floor Area Ratio or 100 sq.m, whichever is less, for non-nuisance, non-
teaching, non-commercial, or non-trading activities for rendering consultancy
based on professional skills such as charted accountant, architect, engineer, town
planner, advocates, doctors (only consultancy) or other similar activities.

In addition to the parking spaces provided, a space at the rate
of 3.5m x 7.5m shall be provided for loading and unloading

For provisions related to setbacks for projected portions at roof level, open spaces and

PUNJAB GOVT. GAZ. (EXTRA), DECEMBER 15, 2025 (AGHN 24, 1947 SAKA) 1707
courtyards, projections and balconies, parking requirements, exemptions, general notes, overall
building requirements, occupant load, clearance zones, part building requirements such as, plinth
levels, spatial requirements for various building components, lighting and ventilation of rooms,
ledges or tand, mezzanine floors, basements, chimneys, parapets, cabins, boundary walls, septic
tanks, office-cum-letter box rooms, meter rooms, staircases and exit routes, corridor dimensions,
ramps, lift and escalator, roof structures, sunken courtyards, check posts or guard rooms or
watch-and-ward cabins, and drainage and sanitation fitments, refer to rules 39 to 43 of these

building rules.
Further, for the provisions of Fire protection, Structural design, Lighting and ventilation,
Electrical and allied installations and Building Materials refer to part VI and for provisions
related to differently-abled, elderly and children refer to part VII of these building rules.
Furthermore, for the provisions of water conservation and management, energy
efficiency, waste management and energy utilization, Energy efficiency, Solar photovoltaic,
Rainwater harvesting and Sustainable waste management refer to part VIII of these building
rules.
Note:-
(a) The plotted Convenient Shopping or Neighbourhood Shopping development shall
be permissible on 18m (60’-0”) wide road subject to the condition that the
maximum area of the site shall not be more than 8000 sq.m. For sites exceeding
8,000 sq.m, the minimum approach road width shall be 24 meters (80’-0”).
(b) In case of commercial plots in a row having openings on both front and rear, then
the ground coverage to be permitted upto 100% of the plot area for Booths or
Shops or SCOs or SCF etc., subject to the conditions that corridors of
appropriate width, as specified above shall be provided in front of each opening.
The FAR, height, number of storeys, clear width of the corridor shall be governed
by the width of the plot and stipulation made under these rules. However, the total
coverage shall continue to be governed by the parking requirements mentioned
above with adequate parking space provided both in front and rear of the site or
plaza as the case may be. The width of such plaza shall not be less than 10
meters.
(c) The depth of parking spaces in front of Booths, Shops, or Shop-cum-Offices
(SCOs) openings shall not be less than 12 meters, except at block junctions where
the front and side of these units intersect and parking is not provided, as
illustrated in Figure 1. In such cases, a minimum distance of 6 meters shall be
maintained between the blocks and this distance may be connected with a
corridor.

Figure 1
(d) Pavement shall be provided adjoining the corridor. The width of such pavement
shall not be less than the minimum corridor width specified in the table above.
(e) The basement shall be restricted under the building including the area under
corridors.
(f) For booths, Double Storey Shop (DSS) or Shop-cum-Offices (SCO’s), having
depth upto 18m and where rear courtyard is not provided, a setback of at least 3
meters from the property lines shall be required.
(g) Approach to upper floors in case of double storeyed shops, shop-cum-shop or
offices can also be permitted from the public corridor.

1708 PUNJAB GOVT. GAZ. (EXTRA), DECEMBER 15, 2025 (AGHN 24, 1947 SAKA)
(h) Approach to basement floors shall be permitted only from within the booth or
shops or shop-cum-shop or offices.
(i) Government commercial site(s) for Retail outlet may be planned as a special
category, irrespective of above rules related to Ground Coverage, FAR, Height,
Number of Floors and these special category sites shall be governed by approved
layout plan or zoning plan or standard architectural control sheets prepared by
the concerned Development Authorities.
(j) No separate fire tender movement shall be mandatory in case of Booths or Shops
or SCOs or SCF maximum upto a height of 18m.
(k) All activities specified in these rules are permitted in commercial sites; except for
school, college, religious buildings, general industries (except green industry) or
factories, storage of hazardous materials, residential apartments and any other
activities that are expressly prohibited under any law; subject to fulfilment of all
applicable requirements in these rules.
(l) In case of Double Storey Shop (DSS) or Shop-cum-Offices (SCO’s), common
staircase or common elevator is permissible maximum for two Double Storey
Shop (DSS) or Shop-cum-Offices (SCO’s) subjected to the condition the minimum
clear width of such staircase shall be 1500 mm.
Provided that if common lift is given, then the minimum clear width of
staircase shall not be less than 1220 mm (4’-0”).
(m) The Maximum floor to floor height of Booth(s) or Shop(s) or Shop-cum-Office(s)
shall not be more than 5000 mm (16’-4”).
(n) Clear height of the basement shall not be more than 4m.
(o) 100% Ground Coverage for all types of Shop-cum-Offices’ shall be subject to the
fulfilment of the following conditions, namely:
(i) The width of the road, abutting the rear side of 'Shop-cum-Offices’, shall be
minimum 20 feet and maximum 80 feet;
(ii) No entry or exit shall be allowed on the service lane or road, abutting the
rear side of the Shop-cum-Offices’;
(iii) There shall be no front of residential plots on the road at the rear side of the
'Shop-cum-Offices’;
(iv) 100% ground coverage shall be mandatory in complete row of Shop-cum-
Offices;
(v) Any service or equipment of the building i.e. air conditioner or gas cylinder
or diesel generator set shall not be installed on the road or plaza at the rear
side of the Shop-cum-Offices'. An undertaking in this regard shall be
obtained from the applicant to ensure the installation of these services or
equipment on the roof;
(vi) It is mandatory to provide the window at the rear side of the Shop-cum-
Offices to ensure the provision of light and ventilation and the sill level of
this window shall be minimum 2.5 feet and this window shall not be used for
any service; and
(vii) if Shop-cum-Offices', where the depth is more than 18m, are proposed back-
to-back with booths, Shops or Double Storey Shop (DSS) or Shop-cum-
Offices (SCO’s) then the distance between them shall be at least 10 meters.
Apart from this, if the applicant wants to propose entry or exit on both sides
then the corridors must be proposed on both sides:
Provided that those commercial colonies or pockets or urban estates
whose layout plans are already approved by the Competent Authority but no
development has taken place on the site i.e. no Shop-cum-Office has been
constructed on the site, in that case, 100% ground coverage can be permitted
in these commercial colonies or pockets or urban estates on the conditions
mentioned above. However, the promoters or development authorities shall
be bound to get the revised layout plans approved by the Competent

PUNJAB GOVT. GAZ. (EXTRA), DECEMBER 15, 2025 (AGHN 24, 1947 SAKA) 1709
Authority. However, a public notice in this regard shall be given in the public
newspapers. Thereafter, the Competent Authority shall take appropriate
decision and if layout plans are revised then 100% ground coverage can be
permitted in those commercial colonies or pockets or urban estates. If the
building plan of any Shop-cum-Office is already approved then the applicant
shall be bound to get the building plans revised.

its urban estates, subject to the fulfilment of the siting parameters. However, in
cases other than those undertaken by the Development Authority, such projects
shall be permitted only in accordance with the provisions of the respective Master
Plan.
(c) For Mixed Land Use projects, the applicable charges shall be the same as those
prescribed for commercial or Group Housing category, whichever is higher
within the respective potential zone.
(d) The relevant norms or provisions, other than above, shall be applicable as per the
component provided for in the Mix Use Project.

(2) Other controls for Industrial Buildings
(i) For low-rise general industry or factory buildings with a site or plot area of 10
acres or more, 15% of the permitted ground coverage may be utilized for high-rise
construction.
(ii) Any newly auctioned industrial site or plot by the development authority, having
an area of 10 acres or more, may also be developed as an Industrial Park, subject
to applicable planning norms and approvals.
(iii) For industrial plots more than 1000 sq.m, 10% of the total plot area to a maximum
of 2500 sq.m., shall be reserved as amenity open space which means an area left
'open to sky' for the welfare or wellbeing of workers or employees or staff. Such
an amenity open space shall have a means of access and shall be so located that it
could be conveniently utilized as such by the persons working in the industry.
(iv) Upto 15 percent of the availed FAR in industrial buildings shall be permissible for
the housing of staff or workers.
(v) *Unlimited F.A.R shall be subject to fulfilment of building controls such as
ground coverage, setback around building, parking norms, light and ventilation,
height, fire and structural safety.
(vi) In case of General Industry or Factory, where corridors and staircases are used
solely for the purpose of access to service areas of machinery, such as
maintenance of machinery or On/Off switch operation, where no habitable space
such as office or seating area or watching cabin or store or toilet etc. are provided,
in such case, the width of such corridors and staircases can be relaxed upto 1000
mm and 1200 mm, respectively, subject to the fulfilment of the other conditions.
These corridor(s) and staircase(s) shall not be considered as fire exit passage or
fire staircase.
(vii) In case of Industrial Estate or Private Industrial Areas or Conglomeration of
Industries opt for central parking then:
(a) Minimum 20 percent parking shall be within the premises of the industry.
(b) The parking requirement if provided in mechanical or common pool area shall
be duly certified by the concerned association by whom it is being maintained.
(c) The share of common parking shall be calculated as per the total area of the
industrial plot duly certified by the concerned agency maintaining that
parking.
(d) In case any false claim regarding common parking is observed then the
industrial plot holder has to provide parking in his own premises.

not permitted to be sold or partially sold.

safety and fire safety norms.
However, for clearance from the Airports
Authority of India or the Indian Air Force, refer to
clause (xi) of rule 40. In cases where the building
height is up to 21 meters, the architect approving the
building plan under self-certification shall be
responsible for ensuring compliance with the
provisions of the above clause; otherwise, the
responsibility shall lie with the competent authority
V. Parking 2 ECS per 100 square meters of the commercial FAR.
For provisions related to setback around building, refer to rule 28, and for provisions related to
setbacks for projected portions at roof level, open spaces and courtyards, projections and
balconies, parking requirements, exemptions, general notes, overall building requirements,
occupant load, clearance zones, part building requirements such as; plinth levels, spatial
requirements for various building components, lighting and ventilation of rooms, ledges or tand,
mezzanine floors, basements, chimneys, parapets, cabins, boundary walls, septic tanks, office-
cum-letter box rooms, meter rooms, staircases and exit routes, corridor dimensions, ramps, lift
and escalator, roof structures, sunken courtyards, check posts or guard rooms or watch-and-ward
cabins, and drainage and sanitation fitments, refer to rules 39 to 43 of these building rules.
Further, for the provisions of Fire protection, Structural design, Lighting and ventilation,
Electrical and allied installations and Building Materials refer to part VI and for provisions
related to differently-abled, elderly and children refer to part VII of these building rules.
Furthermore, for the provisions of water conservation and management, energy
efficiency, waste management and energy utilization, Energy efficiency, Solar photovoltaic,
Rainwater harvesting and Sustainable waste management refer to part VIII of these building
rules.
Note:-
a) The ground coverage includes all built-up areas at the ground level, including the
main structure as well as ancillary facilities such as parking areas, toilets, service
zones, utility rooms, and other supporting buildings.
b) Commercial shops may be rented out but are not permitted to be leased or sold.
c) Completion or partial completion of commercial component shall only be allowed
after the completion of the parking component as per approved plans.
28. Setbacks and open space around the buildings.- For buildings other than residential
Section
180 P.A. plotted or row type buildings or crèche or pre nursery or nursery school or farm house or
No. 11 of
low-rise industry or factory, the set back or open space around the building(s) or outer 1995
open spaces shall be as given in the Table below:
Table 33: Minimum Setback or Open Space Around Buildings
Serial Building Height Range (m) Minimum required Setback or open space all
No. around building (m)

PUNJAB GOVT. GAZ. (EXTRA), DECEMBER 15, 2025 (AGHN 24, 1947 SAKA) 1737
I. Up to first 25 meters ¼ (one-fourth) of the building height, However, it
shall be atleast 3m around the building within site
and 6m from the site boundary.
II. Next 25 meters (above 25m to ⅕ (one-fifth) of the building height + 6.25m for the
50m) first 25m
III. Any height beyond 50 meters ⅙ (one-sixth) of the building height + 11.25m for
the first 50m, however maximum upto 18m
IV. Explanation Let‘s say you‘re planning a 80-meter-tall building.
1. First 25m:
Setback = ¼ × 25m = 6.25 meters
2. Next 25m (25m to 50m):
Setback = ⅕ × 25m = 5 meters
3. Remaining 30m (50m to 80m):
Setback = ⅙ × 30m = 5 meters
Total setback required around the building: 6.25
+ 5 + 5 = 16.25 meters
The maximum required minimum setback is 18m.
Note:-
(i) For buildings exceeding 120 meters in height, an additional setback of 2 meters
shall be required on all sides of the building.
(ii) Where rooms do not derive light and ventilation from the exterior open space, the
width of such exterior open space or distance between two buildings as given
above may be reduced by 1 m subject to a minimum of 3m and a maximum of 8m.
(iii) For high rise buildings and special buildings as specified in rule 31(14) (see
Part4 Fire and Life Safety' of the National Building Code of India, 2016 as
amended from time to time), the approach to the building and open spaces on all
sides shall not be less than 6 m.
(iv) For the plots upto 2025 sq.m area where width of the plot is less than 30.50m
(100'-0") and the maximum height of building is below 21 m excluding the
architectural and symbolism element of religious building like dome, minarets,
sikharas, crucifix etc. from building height, then one side setbacks may be relaxed
subject to the condition that the owner has to submit a fire safety certificate from
the competent authority in this regard.
(v) In addition to setback defined in different category of buildings, for the plots upto
2025 sq.m area where width of the plot is less than 30.50 m (100'-0") and the
maximum height of building is below 21 m excluding the architectural and
symbolism element of religious building like dome, minarets, shikharas, crucifix
etc. from building height then one side setback may be relaxed subject to the
condition that the owner has to submit a fire safety certificate from the competent
authority in this regard.
However, for sites or plots up to 2,025 sq.m in size with roads on more
than one side, a relaxation of 3 meters in setback along the side and rear roads
(excluding the front setback) may be granted, provided that the setback width
shall not be less than 3 meters from the boundary, subject to the following
conditions, namely:
(a) Minimum road width of side or rear road shall not less than 9 m.
(b) The height of the boundary wall including railing for this side shall not be
more than 1 m.
(c) No objection certificate from Fire Department is required for this relaxation.
(d) No entry or exit shall be allowed on such roads, except that a wicket gate of
up to 1.2 meters in width may be permitted.
(vi) In the case of tower-like or recessed buildings, setbacks or open spaces may be
provided in accordance with alternative provisions to Table 33, as detailed
below:

1738 PUNJAB GOVT. GAZ. (EXTRA), DECEMBER 15, 2025 (AGHN 24, 1947 SAKA)
(a) Up to a height of 35 m, with one set-back, the open spaces at the ground level,
shall be not less than 6 m [see Fig. 2(a)];
(b) For heights above 35 m and upto 55 m with one set-back, the open spaces at
the ground level, shall be not less than 9 m [see Fig. 2(b)];
(c) For heights above 55 m and 75 m with two set-backs, the open spaces at the
ground level, shall be not less than 12 m [see Fig. 2(c)];
(d) For heights between 75 m and 120 m with two set-backs, the open spaces at
the ground level, shall be not less than 14 m [see Fig. 2(d)];
(e) For heights above 120 m and above with two set-backs, the open spaces at the
ground level, shall be not less than 16 m [see Fig. 2(e)]; and
(f) The deficiency in the open spaces shall be made good to satisfy Table 33
through the set-backs at the upper levels; these set-backs shall not be
accessible from individual rooms or flats at these levels.
Note: The minimum setback on the ground shall be in proportion to height
specified in the above Table 33.

1742 PUNJAB GOVT. GAZ. (EXTRA), DECEMBER 15, 2025 (AGHN 24, 1947 SAKA)
Note:-
(a) The stilt area shall be used exclusively for car or scooter parking, except for
staircases and lifts.

(b) Basement, if constructed, may be used for parking, household storage, habitable
and consultancy service purposes. Basement shall be counted towards Floor Area
Ratio, if used for habitable purposes.
(c) Front boundary wall shall be optional for providing easy access for parking in
front setback within plot area.
(d) No opening is allowed on party wall of properties.
(e) The maximum permissible ground coverage and Floor Area Ratio is subject to the
fulfilment of the setback, height and parking norms.
(f) A toilet, not exceeding 6 sq.m in area and 2.75 m in height, may be permitted on
the terrace and shall be excluded from the FAR calculation.
(g) The permissible building height shall exclude the parapet, water tank, and mumty.
However, a lift machine room and a toilet may be provided on the terrace floor.
(h) Minimum clear height for habitable room shall be 9’-0”or 2.75 m in every part of
the room.
(i) Open balcony or projection at the 1st or higher floor levels shall not be included
in the covered area. The Maximum permissible width of such balcony shall be 1.2
m for plot sizes upto 250 sq.m and 1.83m for plot sizes above 250 sq.m. However,
the width of the balcony shall not exceed more than half of setback width.
(j) The minimum area of a habitable room shall not be less than 100 sq. feet and one
side not less than 8 ft. and maximum area of a habitable room shall not be more
than 500 sq. feet and one side more than 30’-0”.
(k) The arrangement of habitable rooms and other components of a residential
dwelling unit shall be based on sound principles of planning and design of a
residential building.
(l) Construction of maximum two independent dwelling units on each floor shall be
allowed, provided the site coverage, FAR and height restrictions are adhered to.
(m) Minimum width of staircase in any case shall be 2’-6”.
(n) Minimum ventilation should be 20% of the floor area.
(o) At least 5% of the space to be left open in or around the building shall be used for
plantation.
(p) Professional activity shall be allowed in residential plots and flats on any floor,
subject to the following condition: -
(q) Part of the premises shall be permitted to be used upto a maximum of 50 percent
of Floor Area Ratio or 100 sq.m, whichever is less, for non-nuisance, non-
teaching, non-commercial, or non-trading activities for rendering consultancy
based on professional skills such as charted accountant, architect, engineer, town
planner, advocates, doctors (only consultancy) or other similar activities.
(r) Car parking or porch covered with a fiberglass roof will be permitted in the front
courtyard of the residential house. The stilts shall be permitted for parking
purposes as per norms, subject to condition that the total height achieved is
within the permissible height.
(s) Construction of access ramps within the street or road portion shall not be
allowed. This will be applicable to all categories of buildings.
(t) Clubbing of plots under single ownership for purpose of constructing one unit
with provision of rain water harvesting system & provision of underground water
storage tanks to ensure proper water pressure for supply to upper floors will be
permissible.

terms and conditions mentioned in these regulations. The incentive shall be as under:

(3) Other general control for Independent Floors.- Independent Floors means selling
independent residential floors on residential plots subject to the following:
(i) Ground coverage, FAR, Height and Setbacks shall be as per Residential Plotted. Further,
Rear setback shall be mandatory on plots above 100 sq.m only. Maximum numbers of
floors shall be S+4.
(ii) Provision of a stilt shall be mandatory. The stilt area shall be used exclusively for car or
scooter parking, except for staircases and lifts.

(iii) In case of Independent Floor, common staircase or common elevator is permissible
maximum for two plots subjected to the conditions the minimum clear width of staircases
for buildings shall be 1250 mm.
(iv) The builder or seller of independent floors shall have to obtain permission from Real
Estate Regulatory Authority (RERA), if required.
(v) No Objection Certificate (NOC) regarding Fire safety shall be mandatory.
(vi) Structural Stability Certificate from Structural Engineer shall be mandatory as prescribed
by the Government from time to time.
(vii) For selling Independent Floors, the owner shall be bound to pay charges as applicable to
Group Housing Project less plotted development, if already paid.

duty.

with minimum 40 feet road. Ground coverage and FAR as per Residential
plotted category will be applicable.
(g) Day care centers/ crèches which are above 1000 sq. meters shall be
considered at par with nursery schools.
(h) Play way schools shall attract norms of nursery schools.

(2) Other Controls (for all plot sizes of Nursing Homes or Hospitals):
(i) Upto 25% of the permitted FAR can be utilized for residential use for essential staffs,
dormitory or hostel for attendants of the patients, Crèche.
(ii) Common areas such as waiting halls, reception and fire stairs cases shall be allowed
free from FAR.
(iii) Service floor shall not be counted in FAR.
(iv) Maximum up to 2% of covered area shall be allowed to be used for community space
or crèche or chemist shop or bank counter on Hospital sites and also Medical College
or Nursing and Paramedic institutes sites.
(v) Adequate arrangements for disposal of hospital waste have to be made as per the Bio-
Medical Waste (Management and Handling) Rules, 1998, as amended from time to
time along with the instructions or guidelines issued by the Punjab Pollution Control
Board from time to time.
(vi) The double basement may be allowed, if all other conditions for structural stability,
fire safety, light and ventilation are fulfilled and one of the basements shall be used
exclusively for parking.
(vii) Every building having more than fifteen meters height shall be provided with a
minimum of one lift and total number of such lifts shall be calculated on the basis of
one lift for every 900 square meter floor area above 4 storey.
(a) The size of lift shall not be less than 5‘x8‖
(b) Ramp: The width of the ramp for movement of patients and public shall not be
less than 4‘-6‖ and gradient shall not be less than 1:8.
(viii) Every such building shall have a water storage capacity of 200 liters per bed with an
additional storage of 100 liters per bed where laundry facility is provided.
(ix) Every such building shall have a provision for an alternate power supply and proper
area will be earmarked for installation of a silent generator or any other mode at such
place that it will not cause any inconvenience to the patients, public in general and the
neighborhood.
(x) The provisions contained in ―The persons with Disability Equal Opportunities
protection of Rights & Full participation Act, 1996‖, so far as this relates to planning,
designing and construction of public buildings, guidelines and space standards for
Barrier Free Environment for Disabled & Elderly persons proposed under this Act by
CPWD, Ministry of Urban Affairs & Employment India-1998 or as revised from time
to time shall also be compiled with.

PART VI
GENERAL BUILDING REQUIREMENTS

39. General Planning or Development Norms.- (1) Setback of Projected portion of roof Section 180
P.A. No. 11
level- The projected portion of parapet, flues, ducts, water storage tanks, mumties,
of 1995, 262
minarets, domes, water cooling tanks, lift rooms exceeding 2.75 meters in height shall be (2a) of P.A.
No. 42 of
receded from the facade by a minimum distance equal to their height above roof level, 1976,
240(1)(zzz)
failing which these shall be counted in calculating the height of the building as well as of P.A. No. 3
of 1911, 73
floor area ratio.
of P.A. No. 4
of 1922. However, For the purpose of calculating the height of a building, architectural
elements mean non‑habitable features such as parapets, cornices, ornamental projections,
service chimneys, antennae, and other similar features which are purely decorative or non
functional in nature and do not constitute an additional floor or habitable space or usable
space. Such elements shall not be counted towards the overall permissible height of the
building, provided they conform to the limits specified by the competent authority i.e the
Airport Authority of India, or the Indian Air Force, as the case may be. Any enclosure or
structure on the roof or façade that is used, or is capable of being used, as habitable space
or any non-habitable usable space shall not be treated as an architectural element and
shall be included in the height of the building.
In the case of rooftop installation of photovoltaic solar panels, a clear height of up
to 2.4 meters may be permitted below the panels. This structure shall not be considered in
the calculation of the building's overall height or the Floor Area Ratio (FAR).
(2) Courtyard or Open Space:-
(i) Inner Courtyard for Light and Ventilation.- The minimum area of every closed
or partially closed courtyard of any building upon which habitable rooms abut
shall not be less than 9 sq.m. (nine square meters) and the minimum clear width of
every such courtyard in any direction shall not be less than 2.4 meters or 1/5th of
the average height of the building, whichever is more.
Where only water-closet and bath room are abutting on the inner courtyard,
the size of the inner courtyard shall be in line with the provision for ventilation shaft
as given in sub rule (ii) below.
(ii) Ventilation Shaft.- For ventilating the spaces for water-closets and bath rooms, if
not opening on to outer or interior open spaces, these shall open in to the
ventilation shaft, the size of which shall not be less than the values given below:
Table 64 : Size of Ventilation shaft
Serial Height of building Size of Minimum one
No. (excluding Stilt) ventilation shaft dimension of the shaft
(m2) (m)
I. upto 3 Floors 1.2 0.9
II. upto 4 Floors 2.0 1.2
III. upto 6 Floors 4.0 1.5
IV. upto 8 Floors 5.4 1.8
V. upto 10 Floors 8.0 2.4
VI. Above 10 Floors 9.0 3.0
Note:-
(a) For buildings of height above 30 m, a mechanical ventilation system shall be
installed besides the provision of minimum ventilation shaft.
(b) For fully air conditioned residential or business buildings, the ventilation shaft
need not be insisted upon, provided the air conditioning system works in an
uninterrupted manner, also, provided there is an alternative source of power
supply.
(c) For mechanical ventilation in building shall be as per the provision of National
Building Code of India, 2016 (NBC-2016), amended time to time.

PUNJAB GOVT. GAZ. (EXTRA), DECEMBER 15, 2025 (AGHN 24, 1947 SAKA) 1767
(iii) Outer courtyard or open space.-The minimum width of the outer courtyard or
open space shall be not less than 2.4 m. If the width of the outer courtyard or open
space is less than 2.4 m, it shall not be treated as an outer courtyard or open space
and the provisions of outer courtyard or open space shall not apply. However, if
the depth of the outer courtyard or open space is more than the width, the
provisions of above sub rule (i) shall apply for the open spaces to be left between
the building(s) when any habitable room depends on light and ventilation from

such outer courtyard or open space.
(3) Projection and Balcony.-
Table 65: Projections

whichever is less.

Note:-
(a) The width of the balcony or projection shall not exceed more than half of setback
width.
(b) Provided that projection on door and window shall not exceed 0.91 meters. (3'-0").
(c) Provided further that projection shall not be at a lesser height than 2.06 meters (6'-
9") clear above the plinth level of the building.
(d) In case of corner plots, in any case sunshade not exceeding 0.60 meter (2 feet) on
window opening on to the side shall be permitted at a height not less than 2.06 meters
(6'-9").
(e) If the projection or balcony is provided more than the provision of above Tables, then
the area of the additional projection shall be counted toward F.A.R.
(f) Wrap around continuous balconies will be allowed as long as their area does not
exceed 50% of the total open area, otherwise counted in FAR. In such cases 50% of
open area shall not fall within the shaded area after considering 45 dgree angles
from both sides.
(4) Parking.- (i) Parking of vehicles shall be permissible as given below:-
(a) Surface Parking.
(b) Under stilts of building(s).
(c) Under Basement or Multilevel Basement of the building(s).
Provided that the provisions of the basement shall be as per sub-rule (7)
of rule 43 of these rules.
(d) Under podium of a building(s), the provisions of the podium shall be as
per the National Building Code of India, 2016 (NBC-2016) as amended
from time to time.
(e) Under separate multi-level parking block exclusively for parking,
maximum upto 20 percent of the plot area and such area shall not be
counted towards FAR (Floor Area Ratio) and Ground Coverage subject to
fulfilment of these rules.

1768 PUNJAB GOVT. GAZ. (EXTRA), DECEMBER 15, 2025 (AGHN 24, 1947 SAKA)
(f) Under Multi Level Car Parking within the building block as per the
provisions of these rules.
(ii) A maximum 20% of the total required parking may be allowed as mechanical
parking, except for the residential component in Group Housing. However, it
shall be provided fully automatic in which lower cars do not have to manually be
moved out to take out the cars parked in upper stacks.
Mechanical parking shall be permitted subject to compliance with
mechanical and structural safety and shall be covered with a permanent or
temporary roof structure.
Provided that, to establish automatic stack systems, a brochure
demonstrating the operation of the stacks shall be submitted as part of the
building plan approval application. The completion certificate shall be issued only
after it is verified that the installed stack parking system operates in accordance
with the submitted specifications.
(iii) It is mandatory to provide Electrical Vehicle Charging Infra-structure in all
buildings as per the clauses 4.4.4.3 and 4.4.4.4 of the Punjab Electric Vehicle
Policy-2022 issued by Government of Punjab, Department of Transport,
Notification No. 10/43/2018-2T2 (PF 2022)/ 330 dated the 21st February, 2023.
(iv) Parking under stilts shall be permissible and the clear height of the stilt from the
finished level of the ground floor to the under surface of the beam, joint girders or
any other horizontal structure member shall not be less than 2.4m.
(v) In case parking is provided under stilts or podium or basement it shall not be
counted towards FAR and height for calculating the setback of the building for
the purpose of light and ventilation only.
(vi) Parking spaces shall be paved and clearly marked for different types of vehicles.
(vii) In buildings of mercantile (commercial), industrial (except Information
Technology) and storage type, in addition to the parking spaces provided, a space
at the rate of 3.5 m × 7.5 m, shall be provided for loading and unloading
activities, for each 2000 m2 of floor area or fraction thereof.
(viii) For the provision of car parking spaces, the space standards shall be as under.-
(a) For open parking 23 square meters per equivalent car space.
(b) For ground floor covered parking or Stilt 28 square meters per equivalent
car space.
(c) For basement or podium or multilevel floors: 32 square meter per
equivalent car space.
Provided further that, if the boundary wall is recessed by 6 meters
along the approach road to accommodate parking, then only 11.5 square
meters is required per ECS, as compared to the standard 23 square meters
and this recessed space shall be counted towards the setback subject to the
fulfilment of fire tender movement within the plot.
(ix) Vehicular ramp is mandatory in accordance with sub-rule (18) of rule 43 for
multilevel parking i.e. stilt or basement or podium, if used for vehicular parking.
(x) For plots measuring up to 2025 sq.m, the provision of vehicular ramps for access
to parking areas located in the basement, podium, or upper floors (up to one level)
is not mandatory, provided that car lifts are installed to facilitate vehicle
movement. However, this exemption does not apply to Group Housing
developments or commercial retail or shopping complexes, where ramps are
mandatory regardless of plot size. For plots exceeding 2025 sq.m, the provision of
ramps for vehicular access to all designated parking areas is mandatory.
(xi) In the case of parking spaces provided in basement(s) or multilevel podium(s) or
floor(s) at least two one-way ramps or one two-way ramp shall be provided, with
width and slope in accordance with sub rule (18) of rule 43. These ramps should
preferably be located at opposite ends.

PUNJAB GOVT. GAZ. (EXTRA), DECEMBER 15, 2025 (AGHN 24, 1947 SAKA) 1769
(xii) If there is any doubt regarding the category of any building, for the purpose of
calculation of parking the decision of the Government shall be final. Parking for
the disabled persons shall comply the provisions of ‗Rights of Persons with
Disabilities Act, 2016‘ (Central Act 49 of 2016), National Building Code of India,
2016 and Harmonised Guidelines and Space Standards for Barrier-Free Built
Environment for persons with Disability and Elderly Persons, 2016.
(xiii) Up to 25% of the required parking in case of Information Technology (IT)

Industry and upto 10 % of the required parking for all other categories of
building except residential component in Group Housing, booth, Shops or Shop-
cum-Office (SCO); may be compoundable at the time of completion on the

(b) An annual compoundable increase of 5% shall be applied to the above

(5) Exemptions.-
(i) Exemptions from Ground Coverage:
(a) Canopy or porch maximum upto 500 sq.ft along each entry or exit of the
building,
(b) Atrium or atria, maximum upto 20% of plot area.
(c) Escalator or travellator well, covered sky bridges in high rise buildings,
jogging tracks, swimming pools (open to sky), architectural features or
elements such as, coffers, louvers, end wall projected upto 900 mm;
(d) Pergolas, other sunshade elements, any landscape element;
(e) Fire Staircase, fire towers, ramp;
(f) Podium(s) or multi-storey podium, stilt, separate parking block, basement if
used for parking and the services like sewerage treatment plant, water
treatment plant, garbage collection, electrical sub-station, service ducts.
(g) Service area block having services such as Diesel Generator (DG) room, Air
Conditioning (AC) plant room etc. No habitable rooms are allowed to be
made in any of the items above. If they are, then they will be part of the
Ground Coverage.
(h) Staircases provided in addition to their minimum required shall be exempted
from ground coverage.
(i) Lift Wells

1770 PUNJAB GOVT. GAZ. (EXTRA), DECEMBER 15, 2025 (AGHN 24, 1947 SAKA)
(ii) Exemptions from Floor Area Ratio (FAR):
(a) Canopy or Porch maximum upto 500 sq.ft along each entry or exit of the
building.
(b) Escalator, Atrium or atria, covered sky bridges or intermittent floors as relief
in high rise buildings;
(c) Area on habitable floor for providing services like Heating, Ventilation and
Air Conditioning (HVAC), Mechanical Electrical and Plumbing (MEP)
installations, Janitor rooms, Air Handling Unit (AHU) room, Air
Conditioning (AC) plant room, electric installations, Low Tension (LT) panel
room, Closed Circuit Television (CCTV) room or any other similar services;
(d) Architectural features such as coffers, louvers, end wall projected upto 900
mm, pergolas, other sunshade, elements;
(e) Any landscape element, podium(s) or multistory podium or basement or stilts
of building(s) if used for parking and services like water tank or water works,
sewerage treatment plant, water treatment plant, garbage collection, electrical
sub-station, Diesel Generator (DG) room and service ducts,
(f) Ramp, fire exit stair cases or fire towers, fire refuge area (as per National
Building Code of India, 2016), toilet blocks for visitors or drivers or guards
on ground floor only,
(g) Only one Cupboard in each bedroom upto depth of 0.6 m and 1.8 m length
shall be exempted from Floor Area Ratio.
(h) Any corridor width provided in excess of the dimensions specified below
table shall be excluded from the calculation of FAR:
Table 68: Corridor dimensions
Category of Building Corridor
(i) Residential buildings (e.g., hostels, group housing, 1500 mm
etc.)
(ii) Assembly buildings (e.g., auditoriums, theatres, 2000 mm
cinemas)
(iii) Commercial buildings
2500 mm  Street floor and sales basement
1800mm  Upper sales floor
(iv) Educational buildings and hotels 1500 mm
(v) Hospitals, nursing homes, etc. 2400 mm
(vi) Any other building(s) except industry 1500 mm

(i) Staircases provided in addition to their minimum required shall be exempted
from floor area ratio:
Provided that the area of service shaft shall be taken on one floor:
Provided further that in multi-storey buildings, service floors shall
be allowed after minimum four floors. Maximum of three service floors
shall be allowed in a building. The area of service floors shall not be
counted towards floor area ratio and maximum height of such floors shall
be 2.4 m from floor to bottom of the beam, and in any case, the floor-to-
floor height shall not exceed 3.3 meters.
Any habitable rooms made along with any of the items above will
be taken as part of Floor Area Ratio.

(iii) Exemptions to projections into open spaces or setbacks: Every open space
provided either interior or exterior shall be kept free from any erection thereon
and shall be open to the sky, except as given below:
(a) Cornice, roof or weather shade not more than 0.75 m wide;
(b) Sunshades over windows or ventilators or other openings not more

PUNJAB GOVT. GAZ. (EXTRA), DECEMBER 15, 2025 (AGHN 24, 1947 SAKA) 1771
(c) Canopy not to be used as a sit out with clearance of 1.5 m between the plot
boundary and the canopy;
(d) Projected balcony at higher floors of width not more than as permitted free of
FAR;
However, these projections into open spaces shall not reduce the minimum
required open spaces and hinder the fire tender movement.

(iv) Exemption to accessory use buildings: The following accessory use
buildings may be permitted in the open spaces:
(a) In an existing building, sanitary block of 2.4 m in height subject to a
maximum of 4m2 in the rear open space at a distance of 1.5 m from the rear
boundary may be permitted, where facilities are not adequate.
(b) Parking lock up garages not exceeding 2.4 m in height shall be permitted in
the side or rear open spaces at a distance of 7.5 m from any road line or the
front boundary of the plot;
(c) Suction tank and pump room each up to 2.5 m2 in area.
(d) Meter Room (Power supply) or Diesel Generator(DG) set room, Rain water
harvesting Pit, Check post, Waste or Garbage collection room, Covered
parking for car or motorcycle or cycles, Pump room or Underground tank or
Overhead tank, Cooling Towers for Heating, Ventilation and Air
Conditioning (HVAC) Plant and Diesel Generator(DG) set, Sewer treatment
plant and water treatment plant may be allowed in rear and side setback of
the site subjected to that they cannot hinder the fire tender movement with
minimum 6m setback from the building(s) and the maximum height of these
structure shall not more than 2.75m.
(e) For plot having area one acre and above, Meter Room may be allowed within
area permissible for Watch and Ward Cabins.
40. General Notes.- (i) For projects proposed within the ―Special Area‖ as defined or
Section 180
delineated in the Master Plan or Zonal Plan, the Development regulations of Height, P.A. No. 11
of 1995, 262
Floor Area Ratio, Ground Coverage and any other building controls stipulated in (2a) of P.A.
No. 42 of these rules for general buildings shall be superseded by site-specific Form Based
1976,
240(1)(zzz) Code (FBC) prepared and notified by the Competent Authority under the Punjab
of P.A. No. 3
Regional and Town Planning and Development Act, 1995 or Punjab Municipal Act, of 1911, 73
of P.A. No. 4
1911, Punjab Municipal Corporation Act, 1976, and Punjab Town Improvement Act, of 1922.
1922, as the case may be and the provisions of rules 22 to 27 or rules 31 to 38 of
these rules shall be superseded by relevant Form Based Code (FBC) or Regulating
Plans.
(ii) For projects proposed within the Prohibited and Regulated areas as defined in the
Ancient Monuments and Archaeological Sites and Remains Act, 1958, the
development regulations of Height, Floor Area Ratio, Ground Coverage and any
other controls stipulated in these rules for general buildings shall be superseded by
site specific Heritage Bye-Laws prepared and notified by the Competent Authority or
the National Monuments Authority under the said Act of 1958. No Objection
Certificate (NOC) shall have to be obtained as per the Rules framed under the said
Act by submission of required documents as may be necessary vide rules, including
―Heritage Impact Assessment‖ report, if so, necessitated by the National Monuments
Authority.
(iii) The premises for which building regulations have not been formulated shall be
examined by the Competent Authority on the basis of actual requirements and other
relevant factors. Activities or uses for which these rules are silent shall be governed
by National Building Code of India, 2016, as amended from time to time and where
National Building Code of India, 2016 are silent; Norms or Guidelines of Model
Building Bye laws, 2016 as amended from time to time shall be followed.

1772 PUNJAB GOVT. GAZ. (EXTRA), DECEMBER 15, 2025 (AGHN 24, 1947 SAKA)
(iv) The provision of minimum setbacks for different sizes of plots for all categories of
the plots if not specified in these rules shall be as per the provisions of the Zoning
Plan of the said site.
(v) Area requirements of each type of educational institutions wherever not specified
shall be as per the norms or guidelines issued by the affiliating authority for such
institutions.
(vi) The Punjab Energy Conservation Building Code notified by the Government of
Punjab, Department of New and Renewable Energy vide Notification No. 18/4/16-
61/1856, dated the 24th June, 2016, as amended from time to time, wherever
applicable shall be adhered to the buildings in additions to these rules.
(vii) The setting up of the Petrol pumps, Brick Kiln, Rice Sheller, Stone Crusher, Cinema,
Multiplex, Economical Weaker Section Houses, Affordable Housing, Communication
Towers, Iconic building, Low Density Country Homes, Housing for All, Ancient
Building and any other activity not specifically mentioned in these rules, shall be
governed by the respective policy guidelines or notifications or Acts or rules or norms
of the department of Housing and Urban Development, Punjab or Department of
Local Government, or any other concerned department, as the case may be.
(viii) In addition to these rules, Guidelines or Notifications (amended from time to time)
issued by the Punjab Pollution Control Board (Department of Science, Technology
and Environment, Government. of Punjab) and the Department of Forests and
Wildlife Preservation, Punjab shall be applicable, where ever required.
(ix) No sub-division of the land or site would be subsequently permitted.
(x) Specific approvals shall be obtained from Airports Authority of India, Ministry of
Environment, Forest and Climate Change, Fire Services Department, Pollution
Control Board, designated authorities under the Occupational Safety, Health and
Working Conditions Code, 2020 or the Punjab Cinemas Regulation Act,1952,
Archaeological Survey of India, Heritage Committee, National Highway Authority of
India and any such other authority, as may be applicable. Approval of Fire Services
Department shall be required for buildings of height above 21 m and for such other
buildings or special buildings referred to sub-rule (15) of rule 43.
(xi) In cases of aerodromes where the Colour Coded Zoning Maps have been issued, the
Competent Authority under these rules shall, as per the provisions of the Ministry of
Civil Aviation (Height Restrictions for safeguarding of Aircraft Operations) Rules,
2015 notified vide Notification No. G.S.R. 751 (E), dated the 30th September, 2015
by the Ministry of Civil Aviation and in accordance with the height specifications
provided in such Colour Coded Zoning Maps, approve the building plans up to the
permissible elevation (Average Mean Sea Level), as per the prevailing building rules
or any other law for the time being in force and certify on the sanction plan that the
Floor Space Index or Floor Area Ratio and the related height of the building or
structure is within the permissible elevation as indicated in the Colour Coded Zoning
Maps for the given site:
Provided that no such approval shall be given by the Competent Authority for
sites which lies in approach, take off and transitional areas of an airport or in any
other area, marked in the Colour Coded Zoning Map for the compulsory obtaining of
No Objection Certificate from the designated officer or authorized officer by Ministry
of Civil Aviation:
Provided further that the applicant shall submit all documents as prescribed by
the Airport Authority of India or Indian Air Force, in original to the building plan
approval Competent Authority. The Competent Authority shall approve the plan
without No Objection Certificate from the Airport Authority of India or Indian Air
Force and forward copy of its approval letter along with application and all
documents in original to the Competent Authority of the Airport Authority of India or
Indian Air Force (as applicable) within a period of thirty days of approval. The
Competent Authority shall ensure that the applicants exempted from seeking No

PUNJAB GOVT. GAZ. (EXTRA), DECEMBER 15, 2025 (AGHN 24, 1947 SAKA) 1773
Objection Certificate from Airport Authority of India or Indian Air Force furnish site
coordinates and site elevation certificate issued by the Survey of India in original for
all intended structures and all corners of the plot. The Indian Air Force or Airport
Authority of India shall have power to disapprove the clearance in case of non-
submission of these documents or any discrepancy in the documents. The violations
of the regulations shall be dealt under the Aircraft (Demolition of obstructions caused
by Building and Trees etc.) Rules, 1994. The building plan approval competent

authority shall adhere to all the guidelines issued by the Airport Authority of India or
Indian Air Force in this regard from time to time.
(xii) Approval of Fire Services Department shall be required for all high-rise buildings as
defined in these rules, and for the following categories of special buildings:
(a) hotel, educational, institutional, business, mercantile, industrial, storage,
hazardous and mixed occupancies, where any of these buildings have floor area
more than 500 m2 on any one or more floors;
(b) educational buildings having height 9 m and above;
(c) institutional buildings having height 9 m and above;
(d) all assembly buildings;
(e) buildings having area more than 300 sq.m of incidental assembly occupancy on
any floor and;
(f) buildings with two basements or more, or with one basement of area more than
500 m2.
Note:-
(i) A certificate confirming compliance with Fire and Life Safety provisions,
issued by a Fire Consultant empanelled with the Fire Department, shall
deemed to be considered the Fire No Objection Certificate (Fire N.O.C.).
(ii) The Fire Department shall have the authority to conduct random
inspections to verify the authenticity and validity of such compliance
certificates.
(xiii) In all categories of building excluding residential plots, villas, up to 5% carpet area of
the availed Floor Area Ratio (FAR) may be allocated for commercial use. However,
in case of Group Housing or Studio Apartment and Rental housing or Hostel, this
commercial component may be allocated as convenient shopping as specified in
(xiv)
(xv) Table 4 and
(xvi) Table 7 respectively of rule 22. Such commercial use shall be located within the
main building envelope and shall not be sold or transferred as independent units.
However, in case of large campuses of educational building(s) or institute(s) or
college(s) having an area of 10 acres and above, such commercial use may be
permitted in a separate block, subject to condition that these facilities shall open only
within the premises and shall have no access from the external road.
(xvii) Common indoor sports or games, or common health and fitness facilities, may be
allowed within the premises for all category of buildings. The built-up area of such
common facilities shall be counted at 50% towards the permissible FAR except for
residential plots or farm houses or dwelling units and Commercials such as shopping
mall or Shopping Complex, Shop-cum-Office (SCO) or Shops or Booths. However, if
these facilities are provided on the podium floor, then it shall be exempted from
ground coverage calculations.
(xviii) The clubbing or amalgamation of two or more adjoining plots or sites of same owner
or promoter or applicant may be permitted, subject to the approval of a revised zoning
plan by the Competent Authority. However, bifurcation or fragmentation of a plot or
a site shall not be allowed, except for industrial plots, which are governed by the
relevant departmental policy. However, in case of approved standalone site or project,
the transfer of Change of Land Use (CLU) or approval of the entire site or part
thereof (subject to the fulfilment of minimum area and other siting guidelines) may be
allowed and shall be governed by the relevant policies of the department.
(xix) Creche component may be permitted in all categories of building(s).

1774 PUNJAB GOVT. GAZ. (EXTRA), DECEMBER 15, 2025 (AGHN 24, 1947 SAKA)
(xx) The applicant shall obtain and maintain comprehensive collective insurance coverage
for all building and construction workers engaged at the site, ensuring protection
against injury, disability, or loss of life arising out of or in the course of employment
during the construction period, and shall furnish valid and up-to-date proof of such
insurance upon request during inspections; failure to comply with this requirement
may result in suspension or revocation of construction permits or other enforcement
actions, as determined by the competent authority, the Department of Labour, Punjab,
or the Government, as the case may be.
(xxi) In case of sites or plots already allotted or auctioned by the Government,
Development Authorities, or Government Agencies, the applicable charges shall be
governed by the terms and conditions of the auctioned or as specified in the allotment
letters. However, for sites or plots auctioned after the notification of these building
rules, charges specified in Annexure 22 and Annexure 23 or as specified in the terms
and conditions of auction or allotment letters whichever is higher shall be applicable.

42. Clearance zone for buildings near High Tension Electrical Line – The horizontal Section 180
P.A. No. 11 clearance between the nearest conductor and any part of such building shall, on the basis
of 1995, 262
of maximum deflection due to wind pressure, be not less than: (2a) of P.A.
No. 42 of
1976,
(1) Clearance from buildings of lines of voltage and service lines not exceeding 650 240(1)(zzz)
of P.A. No. 3
V.- of 1911, 73
of P.A. No. 4 (i) An overhead line shall not cross over an existing building as far as possible and
of 1922.
no building shall be constructed under an existing overhead line.
(ii) Where an overhead line of voltage not exceeding 650 V passes above or adjacent
to or terminates on any building, the following minimum clearances from any
accessible point, on the basis of maximum sag, shall be observed; namely:-
(a) for any flat roof, open balcony, varandah roof and lean-to-Roof.
(b) when the line passes adjacent to the building;
a horizontal clearance of 1.2 metre from the nearest point.
(iii) for pitched roof:-
(a) when the line passes above the building, a vertical clearance of 2.5 metre
immediately under the line.
(b) when the line passes adjacent to the building, a horizontal clearance of 1.2
metre.
(iv) Any conductor so situated as to have a clearance less than that specified in sub-
rule (2) shall be replaced with Aerial Bunched Cable and to be attached at suitable
intervals to a bare earthed bearer wire having a breaking strength of not less than
350 kgf.
(v) The horizontal clearance shall be measured when the line is at a maximum
deflection from the vertical due to wind pressure.
(vi) The vertical and horizontal clearances shall be measured as per illustration
provided in figure 3.
Explanation: For the purposes of this regulation, the expression "building" shall
be deemed to include any structure, whether permanent or temporary.

no building shall be constructed under an existing overhead line.

vertical clearance above the highest part of the building immediately under such
line, of not less than-

43. Part Building requirement.– The standard space requirements of various parts of a Section
180 P.A.
building (for all types of buildings-low or high rise), shall be as follows; namely:- No. 11 of
1995, 262
(2a) of P.A.
(1) Plinth – (i) The plinth level for residential plots shall be permitted upto 1500 mm and
No. 42 of
1976, for all other buildings shall be kept as 450 mm from the crown of the adjoining road in
240(1)(zzz)
front or as specified by the Competent Authority from time to time: of P.A. No.
3 of 1911,
Provided that for a detached building, the plinth level may be more than 450 mm: 73 of P.A.

Provided further that the level of front courtyard of any building shall not exceed
1922.
450 mm and the ramp from the front road to the courtyard shall be within the plot
boundary.
(ii) Where a site or plot abuts two or more roads, the plinth level of the building(s) shall
be determined with reference to the road having the higher level.
(2) Interior Court Yard. – Every interior courtyard shall be raised at least 150 mm above
the determining ground level and shall be satisfactorily drained.
(3) Space requirement of different component of a building. – Minimum size, width
and height of different components of building(s), low or high rise is as given below:
